local function getEGOMainColor()
    local c = EGOColors[EGOState.currentEGOColor]
    return c and c.main or Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
end

local function getEGOParticleColors()
    local c = EGOColors[EGOState.currentEGOColor]
    return c and c.particles or EGOColors.YELLOW.particles
end

_G.EGOKillSwitch = function()
    isScriptAlive = false
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and (track.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://138665010911335" or track.Name == "EGOIdle") then
                        track:Stop(0)
                        track:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if child.Name == "EACircle" or child.Name == "OverheadTag" or child.Name == "EGOAttachment" or child.Name == "EgoCircleLogo" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    if EGOState.currentTrack then
        pcall(function()
            EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0)
            EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    if EGOState.emitters then
        for _, emitter in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do
            if emitter and emitter.Parent then emitter:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    if playerGui then
        local h = playerGui:FindFirstChild("EGOHUD")
        if h then h:Destroy() end
        local s = playerGui:FindFirstChild("EgoSpamMini")
        if s then s:Destroy() end
    end
end

local function playCustomSound(id)
    if not isScriptAlive or not hud then return end
    local s = Instance.new("Sound")
    s.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(id)
    s.Volume = 0.55
    s.Parent = hud
    s:Play()
    Debris:AddItem(s, 2)
end

local function createBox(name, text, withDot, interactive)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = name
    box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -4)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Parent = bar
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    stroke.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", box)
    layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding", box)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local dot
    if withDot then
        dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.Parent = box
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    end
    local label = interactive and Instance.new("TextButton") or Instance.new("TextLabel")
    if interactive then label.AutoButtonColor = false end
    label.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.TextColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.Text = text
    label.Parent = box
    return box, label, dot, stroke
end

-- EGO toggle + More button system
local moreBtn, morePanel = nil, nil

local function refreshAllEGOColors()
    local col = getEGOMainColor()
    barStroke.Color = col
    for _, child in ipairs(bar:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then
            local st = child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
            if st then st.Color = col end
            for _, lbl in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                if lbl:IsA("TextLabel") or lbl:IsA("TextButton") then
                    if not lbl.Text:find("Øɴ") and not lbl.Text:find("Øғғ") then
                        -- keep
                    end
                    lbl.TextColor3 = col
                end
            end
        end
    end
    -- Update existing emitters
    local cols = getEGOParticleColors()
    for _, emitter in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do
        if emitter and emitter.Parent then
            pcall(function()
                emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cols[1]),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, cols[2]),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, cols[3]),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cols[4])
                })
            end)
        end
    end
end

local function createMorePanel()
    if morePanel and morePanel.Parent then morePanel:Destroy() end
    morePanel = Instance.new("Frame")
    morePanel.Name = "MoreEGOPanel"
    morePanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 210, 0, 0)
    morePanel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    morePanel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -220, 0, 42)
    morePanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 14)
    morePanel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.15
    morePanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    morePanel.Visible = false
    morePanel.ZIndex = 50
    morePanel.Parent = hud
    Instance.new("UICorner", morePanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local pst = Instance.new("UIStroke", morePanel)
    pst.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    pst.Thickness = 1.4

    local title = Instance.new("TextLabel", morePanel)
    title.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
    title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    title.Text = "🇦🇱  EGO COLORS"
    title.TextColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
    title.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    title.TextSize = 11
    title.ZIndex = 51

    local grid = Instance.new("Frame", morePanel)
    grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 0, 0)
    grid.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 24)
    grid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    grid.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    grid.ZIndex = 51
    local gl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout", grid)
    gl.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 62, 0, 22)
    gl.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
    gl.FillDirectionMaxCells = 3

    local colorOrder = {"YELLOW","GREEN","BLUE","PURPLE","CYAN","ORANGE","PINK","LIME","GOLD","WHITE","TEAL","MAGENTA","SKY"}
    for _, cname in ipairs(colorOrder) do
        local data = EGOColors[cname]
        local b = Instance.new("TextButton", grid)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = data.main
        b.Text = cname
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 16)
        b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        b.TextSize = 9
        b.ZIndex = 52
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            playCustomSound(107406973902030)
            EGOState.currentEGOColor = cname
            refreshAllEGOColors()
            -- force re-setup character to apply new particles
            if player.Character then
                for _, child in ipairs(player.Character:GetDescendants()) do
                    if child.Name == "EGOAttachment" then child:Destroy() end
                end
                EGOState.emitters = {}
                -- trigger setup again by destroying tag briefly
                local head = player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                if head then
                    local tag = head:FindFirstChild("OverheadTag")
                    if tag then tag:Destroy() end
                end
            end
            morePanel.Visible = false
            moreOpen = false
        end)
    end

    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", morePanel)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
end

    end

local function sendChat(msg)
    if #msg > 195 then msg = string.sub(msg, 1, 195) end
    pcall(function()
        if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
            local ch = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
            if ch then ch:SendAsync(msg) end
        else
            local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
            if ev and ev:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest") then
                ev.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(msg, "All")
            end
        end
    end)
end


local function createSpamPanel()
    if spamPanel and spamPanel.Parent then spamPanel:Destroy() end
    spamPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
    spamPanel.Name = "SpamConfigPanel"
    spamPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 200, 0, 0)
    spamPanel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    spamPanel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -210, 0, 42)
    spamPanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 20)
    spamPanel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    spamPanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    spamPanel.Visible = false
    spamPanel.ZIndex = 50
    spamPanel.Parent = hud
    Instance.new("UICorner", spamPanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", spamPanel)
    st.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    st.Thickness = 1.4
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", spamPanel)
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", spamPanel)
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local function makeLabel(txt)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", spamPanel)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 11
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 51
        return l
    end
    local function makeBox(ph, default)
        local b = Instance.new("TextBox", spamPanel)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 34)
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        b.PlaceholderText = ph
        b.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 145)
        b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        b.TextSize = 12
        b.Text = default or ""
        b.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        b.ZIndex = 51
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        return b
    end

    makeLabel("UR. NAME?")
    nameBoxRef = makeBox("Your name...", player.DisplayName or "")
    makeLabel("TARGET NAME")
    targetBoxRef = makeBox("Target name...")

    statusLblRef = Instance.new("TextLabel", spamPanel)
    statusLblRef.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    statusLblRef.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    statusLblRef.Text = "○ READY"
    statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
    statusLblRef.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    statusLblRef.TextSize = 11
    statusLblRef.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    statusLblRef.ZIndex = 51

    -- START / STOP row
    local row = Instance.new("Frame", spamPanel)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.ZIndex = 51
    local rl = Instance.new("UIListLayout", row)
    rl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    rl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    rl.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center

    local startBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    startBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0)
    startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
    startBtn.Text = "START"
    startBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    startBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    startBtn.TextSize = 12
    startBtn.ZIndex = 52
    Instance.new("UICorner", startBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local stopBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    stopBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0)
    stopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 40, 40)
    stopBtn.Text = "STOP"
    stopBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    stopBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    stopBtn.TextSize = 12
    stopBtn.ZIndex = 52
    Instance.new("UICorner", stopBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    -- Spam state
    local spamRunning = false
    local spamThread = nil
    local replies = {
        "TMX MEH GOAT","TMX MEH LEGEND","TMX MEH HALWA","TMX MEH FAN",
        "TMX MEH CAR","TMX MEH KING","TMX MEH BOSS","TMX MEH STAR",
        "TMX MEH FIRE","TMX MEH ICE","RYUK ON TOP","EGO ON TOP",
        "LEAVE MARDE","BHAG JA","ROO MAT","SUN JA"
    }
    local randomEmojis = {"😈","👑","🩸","🤟","🧵","🎯","🤩","🌠","🌟","🌈","🌞","🔥","🇦🇱","🌊","💎","📚","🧊"}

    local function sendMsg(msg)
        if #msg > 195 then msg = string.sub(msg, 1, 195) end
        pcall(function()
            if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
                local ch = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                if ch then ch:SendAsync(msg) end
            else
                local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
                if ev and ev:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest") then
                    ev.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(msg, "All")
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    startBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        playSound(107406973902030)
        if spamRunning then return end
        local sName = cleanName(nameBoxRef.Text)
        local tName = (targetBoxRef.Text ~= "" and targetBoxRef.Text) or "NO ONE"
        spamRunning = true
        startBtn.Text = "● RUNNING"
        startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 50)
        statusLblRef.Text = "● ACTIVE  |  " .. sName .. " → " .. tName
        statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 230, 100)

        spamThread = task.spawn(function()
            local i = 1
            while spamRunning and isScriptAlive and EGOState.spamEnabled do
                local reply = replies[i] or "TMX MEH GOAT"
                local emoji = randomEmojis[math.random(1, #randomEmojis)]
                local header = baseHeader:gsub("AvEK", sName):gsub("🌃", emoji)
                local msg = header .. " [" .. tName .. "] " .. reply
                sendMsg(msg)
                i = i % #replies + 1
                task.wait(1.4)
            end
            spamRunning = false
            if startBtn and startBtn.Parent then
                startBtn.Text = "START"
                startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
            end
            if statusLblRef and statusLblRef.Parent then
                statusLblRef.Text = "○ READY"
                statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
            end
        end)
    end)

    stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        playSound(107406973902030)
        spamRunning = false
        startBtn.Text = "START"
        startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
        statusLblRef.Text = "○ STOPPED"
        statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
    end)
end

    if not isScriptAlive or isTweeningHUD then return end
    isTweeningHUD = true
    playCustomSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.hudVisible = not EGOState.hudVisible

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    if not isScriptAlive then return end
    if dt > 0 then fpsLabel.Text = "Fᴘs: " .. math.floor(1 / dt) end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        pcall(function()
            local pingValue = StatsService.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            pingLabel.Text = "Pɪɴɢ: " .. math.floor(pingValue) .. "ᴍs"
        end)
        local dt = DateTime.now():ToLocalTime()
        local hour = dt.Hour
        local ampm = hour >= 12 and "PM" or "AM"
        if hour > 12 then hour = hour - 12 elseif hour == 0 then hour = 12 end
        timeLabel.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d %s", hour, dt.Minute, ampm)
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- ==========================================
-- CHARACTER SETUP
-- ==========================================
local function setup(character)
    if not isScriptAlive or not character or not character.Parent then return end
    local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 4)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 4)
    local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 4)
    if not head or not humanoid or not hrp then return end
    local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 4) or humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end

    for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "EACircle" or child.Name == "OverheadTag" or child.Name == "EgoCircleLogo" then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end
    for _, child in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do
        if child.Name == "EGOAttachment" then child:Destroy() end
    end
    EGOState.emitters = {}

    if EGOState.currentTrack then
        pcall(function()
            EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0)
            EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy()
        end)
        EGOState.currentTrack = nil
    end

    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "OverheadTag"
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 38)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.9, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    billboard.Parent = head

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = billboard
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8

    -- Spinning circle in overhead
    local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
    circle.Name = "EgoCircleLogo"
    circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    circle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -13)
    circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(8, 8, 12)
    circle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    circle.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local circleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", circle)
    circleStroke.Thickness = 2.2
    circleStroke.Color = getEGOMainColor()

    local circleText = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    circleText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
    circleText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
    circleText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    circleText.Image = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=9933991033&w=150&h=150"
    circleText.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    circleText.Parent = circle

    local ovCycle = {"EE", "13", "HUB 8", "EGO"}
    local ovIdx = 1
    task.spawn(function()
        while isScriptAlive and circle and circle.Parent do
            task.wait(2.8)
            ovIdx = ovIdx % #ovCycle + 1
            if circleText and circleText.Parent then
                -- image logo fixed
            end
        end
    end)

    local rot, hue = 0, 0
    local conn
    conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not isScriptAlive or not circle or not circle.Parent then
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            return
        end
        rot = (rot + 140 * dt) % 360
        circle.Rotation = rot
        hue = (hue + 0.42 * dt) % 1
        local rgb = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.92, 1)
        circleStroke.Color = rgb
        -- circle logo image
        stroke.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    end)

    local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1.5, 0.7, 0)
    divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0.15, 0)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.Parent = frame

    local overheadEGOLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    overheadEGOLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0.54, 0)
    overheadEGOLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.26, 0, 0.02, 0)
    overheadEGOLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    overheadEGOLabel.Text = "Eɢᴏ Eᴍᴘɪʀᴇ: Usᴇʀ"
    overheadEGOLabel.TextColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
    overheadEGOLabel.TextScaled = true
    overheadEGOLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    overheadEGOLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    overheadEGOLabel.Parent = frame

    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0.38, 0)
    nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.26, 0, 0.57, 0)
    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameLabel.Text = "@" .. player.Name
    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    nameLabel.TextScaled = true
    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    nameLabel.Parent = frame

    local renderConn
    renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not isScriptAlive or not character or not character.Parent or not head or not head.Parent then
            renderConn:Disconnect()
            return
        end
        billboard.Enabled = EGOState.tagsEnabled
    end)

    local function createThunder(parent, offsetY, rate, speed, size)
        if not isScriptAlive or not parent or not parent.Parent then return end
        local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Name = "EGOAttachment"
        att.Position = Vector3.new(0, offsetY, 0)
        att.Parent = parent

        local cols = getEGOParticleColors()
        local thunder = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        thunder.Parent = att
        thunder.Texture = "rbxassetid://6101261905"
        thunder.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cols[1]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, cols[2]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, cols[3]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cols[4])
        })
        thunder.LightEmission = 1
        thunder.LightInfluence = 0
        thunder.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 0.6),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, size * 1.4),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, size * 0.9),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        thunder.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.15),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        thunder.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6)
        thunder.Rate = rate
        thunder.Speed = NumberRange.new(speed, speed + 4)
        thunder.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(90, 90)
        thunder.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-250, 250)
        thunder.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
        thunder.ZOffset = 0.4

        local glow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        glow.Parent = att
        glow.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cols[1]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, cols[2]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cols[4])
        })
        glow.LightEmission = 1
        glow.LightInfluence = 0
        glow.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 2.0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, size * 1.4),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.55),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        glow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.5)
        glow.Rate = rate * 0.7
        glow.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 2.5)
        glow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)

        local smoke = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        smoke.Parent = att
        smoke.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cols[4]),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20))
        })
        smoke.LightEmission = 0.2
        smoke.LightInfluence = 0.5
        smoke.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 0.8),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, size * 2.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        smoke.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.8),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        smoke.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8)
        smoke.Rate = rate * 0.4
        smoke.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3)
        smoke.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(45, 45)

        thunder.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        glow.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        smoke.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, thunder)
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, glow)
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, smoke)
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            createThunder(obj, 0, 60, 4, 1.2)
        end
    end
    createThunder(hrp, 0, 70, 5, 1.4)

    local groundAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
    groundAtt.Name = "EGOAttachment"
    groundAtt.Position = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
    groundAtt.Parent = hrp
    local cols = getEGOParticleColors()
    local groundThunder = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    groundThunder.Parent = groundAtt
    groundThunder.Texture = "rbxassetid://6101261905"
    groundThunder.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, cols[1]),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, cols[4])
    })
    groundThunder.LightEmission = 1
    groundThunder.LightInfluence = 0
    groundThunder.Size = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.4),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1.4),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
    })
    groundThunder.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    groundThunder.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7)
    groundThunder.Rate = 75
    groundThunder.Speed = NumberRange.new(3, 6)
    groundThunder.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(110, 110)
    groundThunder.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
    groundThunder.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
    table.insert(EGOState.emitters, groundThunder)

    local idleAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
    idleAnim.Name = "EGOIdle"
    idleAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://138665010911335"
    local idleTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(idleAnim)
    idleTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
    idleTrack.Looped = true
    EGOState.currentTrack = idleTrack

    humanoid.Running:Connect(function(speed)
        if not isScriptAlive then return end
        if speed < 0.5 then
            if EGOState.animEnabled and not idleTrack.IsPlaying then idleTrack:Play(0.1) end
        else
            if idleTrack.IsPlaying then idleTrack:Stop(0.1) end
        end
    end)
    if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.1 and EGOState.animEnabled then
        idleTrack:Play(0.1)
    end
end

local function attachCharacterTracks(character)
    if not isScriptAlive or not character then return end
    task.spawn(setup, character)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        local currentCharacter = player.Character
        if currentCharacter and currentCharacter.Parent then
            local head = currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head and not head:FindFirstChild("OverheadTag") then
                task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, currentCharacter)
            end
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
end)

if player.Character then task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, player.Character) end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, char)
end)

_G.EGO_WallMode="BOYS" -- default boys
_G.EGO_WallRandom=false
_G.EGO_WallRandomToken=0
_G.EGO_WallList=WallBoys
_G.EGO_WallIndex=1

local WallImg=Instance.new("ImageLabel")
WallImg.Name="EGO_Wallpaper"
WallImg.Parent=Main
WallImg.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
WallImg.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
WallImg.BackgroundTransparency=1
WallImg.BorderSizePixel=0
WallImg.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
WallImg.ImageTransparency=0.12
WallImg.ZIndex=0
Instance.new("UICorner",WallImg).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)
_G.EGO_Wallpaper=WallImg

local function ApplyWallpaper(id)
	if not id or not WallImg then return end
	id=tostring(id):gsub("%D","")
	if id=="" then return end
	pcall(function()
		WallImg.Image="rbxthumb://type=Asset&id="..id.."&w=420&h=420"
	end)
end

local function CurrentWallPool()
	if _G.EGO_WallMode=="GIRLS" then return WallGirls end
	return WallBoys
end

local function SetWallMode(mode)
	if mode~="BOYS" and mode~="GIRLS" then return end
	_G.EGO_WallMode=mode
	_G.EGO_WallList=CurrentWallPool()
	_G.EGO_WallIndex=1
	if #_G.EGO_WallList>0 then
		ApplyWallpaper(_G.EGO_WallList[1])
	end
end

local function PickRandomWall()
	local pool=CurrentWallPool()
	if not pool or #pool==0 then return end
	local id=pool[math.random(1,#pool)]
	ApplyWallpaper(id)
end

local function StopWallRandom()
	_G.EGO_WallRandom=false
	_G.EGO_WallRandomToken=(_G.EGO_WallRandomToken or 0)+1
end

local function StartWallRandom()
	-- cancel any previous random loop, then start a new one
	_G.EGO_WallRandomToken=(_G.EGO_WallRandomToken or 0)+1
	local myToken=_G.EGO_WallRandomToken
	_G.EGO_WallRandom=true
	-- apply one immediately so user sees change
	pcall(PickRandomWall)
	task.spawn(function()
		while _G.EGO_WallRandom and myToken==_G.EGO_WallRandomToken do
			task.wait(3)
			if not (_G.EGO_WallRandom and myToken==_G.EGO_WallRandomToken) then break end
			if WallImg and WallImg.Parent then
				pcall(PickRandomWall)
			end
		end
	end)
end

-- start: locked save OR gender-based starter pool OR random
do
	local savedMode=_G.EGO_CFG and _G.EGO_CFG.wallMode
	local savedId=_G.EGO_CFG and _G.EGO_CFG.wallId
	local locked=_G.EGO_CFG and _G.EGO_CFG.wallLocked
	local g=UserGender or (_G.EGO_CFG and _G.EGO_CFG.gender)
	-- Boys starter picks (1-based): 12,16,17,9
	local boysStart={12,16,17,9}
	-- Girls starter picks (1-based): 3,10,2,6
	local girlsStart={3,10,2,6}
	if locked and savedId and tostring(savedId)~="" then
		if savedMode=="GIRLS" or savedMode=="BOYS" then _G.EGO_WallMode=savedMode end
		_G.EGO_WallLocked=true
		_G.EGO_WallList=CurrentWallPool()
		ApplyWallpaper(savedId, false)
	else
		_G.EGO_WallLocked=false
		if g=="FEMALE" then
			_G.EGO_WallMode="GIRLS"
			_G.EGO_WallList=WallGirls
			local picks={}
			for _,idx in ipairs(girlsStart) do
				if WallGirls[idx] then table.insert(picks, WallGirls[idx]) end
			end
			if #picks==0 then picks=WallGirls end
			ApplyWallpaper(picks[math.random(1,#picks)], false)
		else
			_G.EGO_WallMode="BOYS"
			_G.EGO_WallList=WallBoys
			local picks={}
			for _,idx in ipairs(boysStart) do
				if WallBoys[idx] then table.insert(picks, WallBoys[idx]) end
			end
			if #picks==0 then picks=WallBoys end
			ApplyWallpaper(picks[math.random(1,#picks)], false)
		end
	end
end

_G.EGO_ApplyWallpaper=ApplyWallpaper
_G.EGO_SetWallMode=SetWallMode
_G.EGO_PickRandomWall=PickRandomWall
_G.EGO_StartWallRandom=StartWallRandom
_G.EGO_StopWallRandom=StopWallRandom
_G.EGO_WallBoys=WallBoys
_G.EGO_WallGirls=WallGirls

PFav=createPage()local RefreshFavsPage local function tab(name,p)
local b=Insta("TextButton",Sidebar)
b.Size=UDim2.new(1,-6,0,32)
b.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
b.BackgroundTransparency=0.88
b.Text=name
b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255)
b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
b.TextSize=12
b.AutoButtonColor=false
Insta("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
local st=Instance.new("UIStroke",b)
st.Thickness=1
st.Color=GetColor()
st.Transparency=0.55
b.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
	pcall(function()
		TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{BackgroundTransparency=0.72}):Play()
		TweenService:Create(st,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{Transparency=0.25}):Play()
	end)
end)
b.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
	pcall(function()
		if not b:GetAttribute("EGO_ActiveTab") then
			TweenService:Create(b,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{BackgroundTransparency=0.88}):Play()
			TweenService:Create(st,TweenInfo.new(0.12),{Transparency=0.55}):Play()
		end
	end)
end)
b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	for _,v in pairs(Pages:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then v.Visible=false end
	end
	p.Visible=true
	for _,child in ipairs(Sidebar:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextButton") then
			child:SetAttribute("EGO_ActiveTab",false)
			child.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
			child.BackgroundTransparency=0.88
			child.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(245,245,255)
			local s=child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if s then s.Color=GetColor(); s.Transparency=0.55 end
		end
	end
	b:SetAttribute("EGO_ActiveTab",true)
	b.BackgroundColor3=GetColor()
	b.BackgroundTransparency=0.35
	b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10,10,26)
	local s=b:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	if s then s.Color=GetColor(); s.Transparency=0.2 end
	SearchBar.Text=""
	suggestionFrame.Visible=false
	suggestionFrame.Size=UDim2.new(0.48,0,0,0)
	if p==P14 then OnJoinGroupTabOpened() end
	local layout=p:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
	if layout then p.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,layout.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+20) end
	p.AutomaticCanvasSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
end)
end

-- ========== WALLPAPER TAB UI (P12) — thumbnails + random preview ==========
task.spawn(function()
	task.wait(0.15)
	pcall(function()
		if not P12 then warn("[EGO] P12 missing — wallpaper tab skipped") return end
		for _,ch in ipairs(P12:GetChildren()) do
			if not ch:IsA("UIListLayout") and not ch:IsA("UIPadding") then ch:Destroy() end
		end

		local function wallThumbUrl(id)
			id=tostring(id or ""):gsub("%D","")
			if id=="" then return "" end
			return "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id="..id.."&w=150&h=150"
		end

		local function wLabel(txt, size)
			local l=Instance.new("TextLabel",P12)
			l.Size=UDim2.new(1,-10,0,size or 20)
			l.BackgroundTransparency=1
			l.Text=txt
			l.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,100)
			l.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
			l.TextSize=13
			l.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			return l
		end

		local function wBtn(txt, color, cb)
			local b=Instance.new("TextButton",P12)
			b.Size=UDim2.new(1,-10,0,30)
			b.BackgroundColor3=color or Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0)
			b.BackgroundTransparency=0.12
			b.Text=txt
			b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10,10,26)
			b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
			b.TextSize=12
			b.AutoButtonColor=false
			Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() pcall(cb,b) end)
			return b
		end

		-- grid of real wallpaper image buttons
		local function addThumbGrid(ids, accent)
			local grid=Instance.new("Frame",P12)
			grid.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,0)
			grid.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
			grid.BackgroundTransparency=1
			local gl=Instance.new("UIGridLayout",grid)
			gl.CellSize=UDim2.new(0,72,0,72)
			gl.CellPadding=UDim2.new(0,6,0,6)
			gl.FillDirectionMaxCells=4
			gl.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
			gl:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
				grid.Size=UDim2.new(1,-8,0,gl.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+4)
			end)
			for i,id in ipairs(ids or {}) do
				local cell=Instance.new("TextButton",grid)
				cell.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(12,10,22)
				cell.BackgroundTransparency=0.15
				cell.Text=""
				cell.AutoButtonColor=false
				cell.ClipsDescendants=true
				Instance.new("UICorner",cell).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
				local cs=Instance.new("UIStroke",cell)
				cs.Thickness=1.2
				cs.Color=accent
				cs.Transparency=0.35
				local img=Instance.new("ImageLabel",cell)
				img.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				img.BackgroundTransparency=1
				img.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
				img.Image=wallThumbUrl(id)
				img.ZIndex=1
				Instance.new("UICorner",img).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,8)
				local glow=Instance.new("Frame",cell)
				glow.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,16)
				glow.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-16)
				glow.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
				glow.BackgroundTransparency=0.45
				glow.BorderSizePixel=0
				glow.ZIndex=2
				local num=Instance.new("TextLabel",glow)
				num.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				num.BackgroundTransparency=1
				num.Text=tostring(i)
				num.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
				num.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
				num.TextSize=10
				num.ZIndex=3
				cell.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						TweenService:Create(cs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Transparency=0.05,Thickness=2}):Play()
					end)
				end)
				cell.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						TweenService:Create(cs,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Transparency=0.35,Thickness=1.2}):Play()
					end)
				end)
				cell.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					if _G.EGO_StopWallRandom then _G.EGO_StopWallRandom() else _G.EGO_WallRandom=false end
					if randBtn then
						randBtn.Text="🎲 RANDOM WALLPAPER"
						randBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75)
					end
					if _G.EGO_ApplyWallpaper then _G.EGO_ApplyWallpaper(id, true) end
				end)
			end
			return grid
		end

		wLabel("── 🖼️ WALLPAPER STUDIO ──", 22)

		local modeBoys=wBtn("✓ BOYS MODE", Color3.fromRGB(80,140,255), function() end)
		local modeGirls=wBtn("○ GIRLS MODE", Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75), function() end)

		local function refreshModeBtns()
			if _G.EGO_WallMode=="BOYS" then
				modeBoys.Text="✓ BOYS MODE"
				modeBoys.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(80,140,255)
				modeGirls.Text="○ GIRLS MODE"
				modeGirls.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75)
			else
				modeGirls.Text="✓ GIRLS MODE"
				modeGirls.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,120,180)
				modeBoys.Text="○ BOYS MODE"
				modeBoys.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75)
			end
		end

		local randBtn=wBtn("🎲 RANDOM WALLPAPER", Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75), function() end)
		local function setRandomBtnState(on)
			if on then
				randBtn.Text="● RANDOM ON (3s)"
				randBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,180,100)
			else
				randBtn.Text="🎲 RANDOM WALLPAPER"
				randBtn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(55,55,75)
			end
		end

		modeBoys.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if _G.EGO_SetWallMode then _G.EGO_SetWallMode("BOYS") end
			refreshModeBtns()
			if _G.EGO_WallRandom and _G.EGO_PickRandomWall then pcall(_G.EGO_PickRandomWall) end
		end)
		modeGirls.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if _G.EGO_SetWallMode then _G.EGO_SetWallMode("GIRLS") end
			refreshModeBtns()
			if _G.EGO_WallRandom and _G.EGO_PickRandomWall then pcall(_G.EGO_PickRandomWall) end
		end)
		refreshModeBtns()

		-- Random popup with live fast-cycling image previews
		randBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local pop=Instance.new("ScreenGui")
			pop.Name="EGO_WallRandPop"
			pop.ResetOnSpawn=false
			pop.IgnoreGuiInset=true
			pop.DisplayOrder=10020
			pop.Parent=CoreGui
			local alive=true
			pop.Destroying:Connect(function() alive=false end)

			local bg=Instance.new("Frame",pop)
			bg.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
			bg.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
			bg.BackgroundTransparency=0.4

			local card=Instance.new("Frame",bg)
			card.Size=UDim2.new(0,340,0,230)
			card.Position=UDim2.new(0.5,-170,0.5,-115)
			card.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10,8,20)
			Instance.new("UICorner",card).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,14)
			local cst=Instance.new("UIStroke",card)
			cst.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0)
			cst.Thickness=2

			local title=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
			title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,26)
			title.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,8)
			title.BackgroundTransparency=1
			title.Text="🎲 RANDOM WALLPAPER"
			title.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,230,150)
			title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
			title.TextSize=15

			local sub=Instance.new("TextLabel",card)
			sub.Size=UDim2.new(1,-20,0,16)
			sub.Position=UDim2.new(0,10,0,32)
			sub.BackgroundTransparency=1
			sub.Text="Preview cycles fast · pick a mode"
			sub.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(160,160,185)
			sub.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham
			sub.TextSize=11

			local function makePreviewBtn(xScale, label, pool, mode, accent)
				local btn=Instance.new("TextButton",card)
				btn.Size=UDim2.new(0,148,0,130)
				btn.Position=UDim2.new(0,xScale,0,56)
				btn.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(14,12,28)
				btn.Text=""
				btn.AutoButtonColor=false
				btn.ClipsDescendants=true
				Instance.new("UICorner",btn).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
				local bst=Instance.new("UIStroke",btn)
				bst.Thickness=1.6
				bst.Color=accent
				bst.Transparency=0.25

				local img=Instance.new("ImageLabel",btn)
				img.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				img.BackgroundTransparency=1
				img.ScaleType=Enum.ScaleType.Crop
				img.ImageTransparency=0.05
				img.ZIndex=1
				Instance.new("UICorner",img).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)

				local overlay=Instance.new("Frame",btn)
				overlay.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,28)
				overlay.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-28)
				overlay.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
				overlay.BackgroundTransparency=0.35
				overlay.BorderSizePixel=0
				overlay.ZIndex=2

				local lbl=Instance.new("TextLabel",overlay)
				lbl.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0)
				lbl.BackgroundTransparency=1
				lbl.Text=label
				lbl.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
				lbl.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
				lbl.TextSize=12
				lbl.ZIndex=3

				-- fast cycle illusion
				if pool and #pool>0 then
					img.Image=wallThumbUrl(pool[1])
					task.spawn(function()
						local i=1
						while alive and img and img.Parent do
							i=i%#pool+1
							img.Image=wallThumbUrl(pool[i])
							task.wait(0.18)
						end
					end)
				end

				btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						TweenService:Create(bst,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Transparency=0,Thickness=2.2}):Play()
						TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Size=UDim2.new(0,152,0,134)}):Play()
					end)
				end)
				btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
					pcall(function()
						TweenService:Create(bst,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Transparency=0.25,Thickness=1.6}):Play()
						TweenService:Create(btn,TweenInfo.new(0.1),{Size=UDim2.new(0,148,0,130)}):Play()
					end)
				end)
				btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					alive=false
					if _G.EGO_SetWallMode then _G.EGO_SetWallMode(mode) end
					refreshModeBtns()
					if _G.EGO_StartWallRandom then
						_G.EGO_StartWallRandom()
					else
						_G.EGO_WallRandom=true
						if _G.EGO_PickRandomWall then pcall(_G.EGO_PickRandomWall) end
					end
					setRandomBtnState(true)
					pop:Destroy()
				end)
				return btn
			end

			local boysPool=_G.EGO_WallBoys or {}
			local girlsPool=_G.EGO_WallGirls or {}
			makePreviewBtn(16, "👦 BOYS RANDOM", boysPool, "BOYS", Color3.fromRGB(80,140,255))
			makePreviewBtn(176, "👧 GIRLS RANDOM", girlsPool, "GIRLS", Color3.fromRGB(255,120,180))

			local cancel=Instance.new("TextButton",card)
			cancel.Size=UDim2.new(0,90,0,24)
			cancel.Position=UDim2.new(1,-100,1,-30)
			cancel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(70,35,40)
			cancel.Text="CLOSE"
			cancel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
			cancel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold
			cancel.TextSize=11
			Instance.new("UICorner",cancel).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
			cancel.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				alive=false
				pop:Destroy()
			end)
		end)

		wBtn("■ STOP RANDOM", Color3.fromRGB(140,50,50), function()
			if _G.EGO_StopWallRandom then
				_G.EGO_StopWallRandom()
			else
				_G.EGO_WallRandom=false
			end
			setRandomBtnState(false)
		end)

		wLabel("── BOYS WALLPAPERS ──", 20)
		addThumbGrid(_G.EGO_WallBoys or {}, Color3.fromRGB(80,140,255))

		wLabel("── GIRLS WALLPAPERS ──", 20)
		addThumbGrid(_G.EGO_WallGirls or {}, Color3.fromRGB(255,120,180))

		local lay=P12:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
		if lay then
			P12.CanvasSize=UDim2.new(0,0,0,lay.AbsoluteContentSize.Y+40)
		end
		
print("✅ WALLPAPER TAB READY")
end)
end)

-- ========== STEAL AN EGG TAB (P9) ==========
task.defer(function()
	task.wait(0.4)
	pcall(function()
		if not P9 then return end
		local layout=P9:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIListLayout")
		if not layout then
			layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout",P9)
			layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,6)
			layout.SortOrder=Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		end
		for _,c in ipairs(P9:GetChildren()) do
			if not c:IsA("UIListLayout") and not c:IsA("UIPadding") then c:Destroy() end
		end
		local title=Instance.new("TextLabel",P9)
		title.Size=UDim2.new(1,-10,0,24)

function ConvertToEGOFont(text)local result=""for i=1,#text do local char=text:sub(i,i)result=result..(smallCapsMap[char]or char)end return result end local function GetPostStorageKey(userId)return"EGO_EMPIRE_POST_"..tostring(userId)..".txt"end local function SaveCustomPost(userId,post)local saved=false local key=GetPostStorageKey(userId)pcall(function()if writefile then writefile(key,tostring(post))saved=true end end)_G.EGO_CustomPosts=_G.EGO_CustomPosts or{}_G.EGO_CustomPosts[tostring(userId)]=post if not saved then saved=true end return saved end local function LoadCustomPost(userId)local loaded=nil local key=GetPostStorageKey(userId)pcall(function()if isfile and isfile(key)and readfile then local data=readfile(key)if data and#data>0 then loaded=data end elseif readfile then local ok,data=pcall(readfile,key)if ok and data and#data>0 then loaded=data end end end)if not loaded then _G.EGO_CustomPosts=_G.EGO_CustomPosts or{}loaded=_G.EGO_CustomPosts[tostring(userId)]end return loaded end local customPosts={}local loadedPost=LoadCustomPost(player.UserId)if loadedPost then customPosts[player.UserId]=loadedPost end task.spawn(function()
	local rot=0
	while T and T.Parent do
		local h=(tick()*0.4)%1
		local gold=Color3.fromRGB(255,215,0)
		local rgb=Color3.fromHSV(h,0.8,1)
		if FloatingGlowRef and FloatingGlowRef.Parent then
			FloatingGlowRef.Color=gold:Lerp(rgb,0.45)
			FloatingGlowRef.Thickness=2.4+math.sin(tick()*2)*0.7
		end
		if ringSt and ringSt.Parent then
			ringSt.Color=Color3.fromHSV((h+0.25)%1,0.75,1)
		end
		rot=(rot+1.2)%360
		if ring and ring.Parent then ring.Rotation=rot end
		task.wait(0.04)

EGO_BAN_LIST = {
    "rajputtime7",
    "UVZEBCUSGTJ",
    "uvzebcusgtj",
}
local function IsBannedUser()
    local function clean(s)
        s = tostring(s or ""):lower()
        s = s:gsub("%s+", ""):gsub("_", ""):gsub("%-", "")
        return s
    end
    local n = clean(player.Name)
    local d = clean(player.DisplayName)
    for _, banned in ipairs(EGO_BAN_LIST) do
        local b = clean(banned)
        if n == b or d == b or n:find(b, 1, true) or d:find(b, 1, true) then
            return true
        end
    end
    return false
end

function IsEAName()
    local function norm(s)
        s = tostring(s or ""):lower()
        s = s:gsub("%s+", "")
        return s
    end
    local n = norm(player.Name)
    local d = norm(player.DisplayName)
    -- any 13_ / ea prefix style (13_, ea, _ea, etc.)
    local patterns = {"13_", "_ea", "ea-", "-ea"}
    for _, p in ipairs(patterns) do
        if n:find(p, 1, true) or d:find(p, 1, true) then return true end
    end
    -- starts with ea + digit/underscore/letter boundary
    if n:match("^ea[_%-]") or d:match("^ea[_%-]") then return true end
    if n:match("^ea%d") or d:match("^ea%d") then return true end
    -- display with 13_ mixed case already lowered
    if n:find("13_", 1, true) or d:find("13_", 1, true) then return true end
    return false
end

        if enter then task.spawn(tryUnlock) end
    end)
end

-- ==========================================
task.spawn(function()
    task.wait(3)
    local EGOCode = [========[
-- LocalScript
-- NARAK GANG EGO - Red Star Removed + Spinning RGB Circle

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local StatsService = game:GetService("Stats")
local TweenService = game:GetService("TweenService")
local Debris = game:GetService("Debris")
local TextChatService = game:GetService("TextChatService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local player = Players.LocalPlayer

local playerGui = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui", 10) or player:FindFirstChildOfClass("PlayerGui")

-- ========================================================
-- ANTI-OVERLAP & RE-EXECUTION SYSTEM
-- ========================================================
if _G.EGOKillSwitch then
    pcall(_G.EGOKillSwitch)
end

_G.EGOState = {
    tagsEnabled = true,
    EGOEnabled = true,
    animEnabled = true,
    spamEnabled = false,
    hudVisible = true,
    emitters = {},
    currentTrack = nil
}
local EGOState = _G.EGOState
local isScriptAlive = true

_G.EGOKillSwitch = function()
    isScriptAlive = false
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if humanoid then
            local animator = humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if animator then
                for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if track.Animation and (track.Animation.AnimationId == "rbxassetid://138665010911335" or track.Name == "EGOIdle") then
                        track:Stop(0)
                        track:Destroy()
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        for _, child in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if child.Name == "EACircle" or child.Name == "OverheadTag" or child.Name == "EGOAttachment" or child.Name == "EgoCircleLogo" then
                child:Destroy()
            end
        end
    end
    if EGOState.currentTrack then
        pcall(function()
            EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0)
            EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy()
        end)
    end
    if EGOState.emitters then
        for _, emitter in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do
            if emitter and emitter.Parent then emitter:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    if playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("EGOHUD") then
        playerGui.EGOHUD:Destroy()
    end
end

-- =====================
-- HUD BAR LAYOUT
-- =====================
if playerGui and playerGui:FindFirstChild("EGOHUD") then
    playerGui.EGOHUD:Destroy()
end

local hud = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
hud.Name = "EGOHUD"
hud.ResetOnSpawn = false
hud.IgnoreGuiInset = true
hud.DisplayOrder = 9999999
hud.Parent = playerGui

local function playCustomSound(soundId)
    if not isScriptAlive or not hud then return end
    local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
    sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://" .. tostring(soundId)
    sound.Volume = 0.6
    sound.Parent = hud
    sound:Play()
    Debris:AddItem(sound, 3)
end

local function createBox(name, text, withDot, interactive)
    local box = Instance.new("Frame")
    box.Name = name
    box.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, -4)
    box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
    box.BorderSizePixel = 0
    box.Parent = bar
    Instance.new("UICorner", box).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 4)
    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", box)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
    stroke.Thickness = 1
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", box)
    layout.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    layout.VerticalAlignment = Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 3)
    local padding = Instance.new("UIPadding", box)
    padding.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 6)
    padding.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 6)
    local dot
    if withDot then
        dot = Instance.new("Frame")
        dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5)
        dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100)
        dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
        dot.Parent = box
        Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
    end
    local label = interactive and Instance.new("TextButton") or Instance.new("TextLabel")
    if interactive then label.AutoButtonColor = false end
    label.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.X
    label.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    label.TextSize = 10
    label.Text = text
    label.Parent = box
    return box, label, dot
end

local spamText = "SCRIPTER ]> NARAK GANG💢//THE FAMOUS LEADER ØF NARAK GANG💢//EGO WALO MERE PYARE BACHE💢// () DEDO 💢// NAME EGO !? but REALITY [RND]💢/WORST GANG RND EMPIRE💢..."

local function sendChatMessage(message)
    if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
        local targetChannel = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
        if targetChannel then targetChannel:SendAsync(message) end
    else
        local sayMsgReq = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents") and ReplicatedStorage.DefaultChatSystemChatEvents:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest")
        if sayMsgReq then sayMsgReq:FireServer(message, "All") end
    end
end

local _, spamLabel, spamDot = createBox("SpamToggle", "Sᴘᴀᴍ Øғғ", true, true)
spamLabel.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not isScriptAlive then return end
    playCustomSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.spamEnabled = not EGOState.spamEnabled
    spamLabel.Text = EGOState.spamEnabled and "Sᴘᴀᴍ Øɴ" or "Sᴘᴀᴍ Øғғ"
    spamDot.BackgroundColor3 = EGOState.spamEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 100) or Color3.fromRGB(120, 80, 0)
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        if EGOState.spamEnabled then pcall(sendChatMessage, spamText) end
        task.wait(2.6)
    end
end)

-- HUD LOGO TWEEN (click to hide/show bar)
local isTweeningHUD = false
local currentRotation = 0
logoClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not isScriptAlive or isTweeningHUD then return end
    isTweeningHUD = true
    playCustomSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.hudVisible = not EGOState.hudVisible
    local targetSliderPos = EGOState.hudVisible and UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0) or UDim2.new(0, 850, 0, 0)
    local slideTween = TweenService:Create(sliderFrame, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {Position = targetSliderPos})
    slideTween:Play()
    slideTween.Completed:Connect(function() isTweeningHUD = false end)
end)

    if not isScriptAlive then return end
    if dt > 0 then fpsLabel.Text = "Fᴘs: " .. math.floor(1 / dt) end
end)

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        pcall(function()
            local pingValue = StatsService.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            pingLabel.Text = "Pɪɴɢ: " .. math.floor(pingValue) .. "ᴍs"
        end)
        local dt = DateTime.now():ToLocalTime()
        local hour = dt.Hour
        local ampm = hour >= 12 and "PM" or "AM"
        if hour > 12 then hour = hour - 12 elseif hour == 0 then hour = 12 end
        timeLabel.Text = string.format("%02d:%02d %s", hour, dt.Minute, ampm)
        task.wait(1)
    end
end)

-- ==========================================
-- CHARACTER SETUP (EGO & OVERHEAD TAG)
-- ==========================================
local function setup(character)
    if not isScriptAlive or not character or not character.Parent then return end

    local head = character:WaitForChild("Head", 4)
    local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 4)
    local hrp = character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart", 4)
    if not head or not humanoid or not hrp then return end
    local animator = humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator", 4) or humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end

    -- Clean old tags
    for _, child in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do
        if child.Name == "EACircle" or child.Name == "OverheadTag" or child.Name == "EgoCircleLogo" then
            child:Destroy()
        end
    end

    if EGOState.currentTrack then
        pcall(function()
            EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0)
            EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy()
        end)
        EGOState.currentTrack = nil
    end

    -- ===== OVERHEAD TAG =====
    local billboard = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    billboard.Name = "OverheadTag"
    billboard.Size = UDim2.new(0, 150, 0, 38)
    billboard.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 2.9, 0)
    billboard.AlwaysOnTop = true
    billboard.MaxDistance = math.huge
    billboard.Parent = head

    local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
    frame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
    frame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    frame.Parent = billboard
    Instance.new("UICorner", frame).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local stroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", frame)
    stroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
    stroke.Thickness = 1.8

    -- ===== SPINNING RGB CIRCLE (replaces red star) =====
    local circle = Instance.new("Frame")
    circle.Name = "EgoCircleLogo"
    circle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 0, 26)
    circle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0.5, -13)
    circle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10)
    circle.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    circle.BorderSizePixel = 0
    circle.Parent = frame
    Instance.new("UICorner", circle).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)

    local circleStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke", circle)
    circleStroke.Thickness = 2.2
    circleStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)

    local circleText = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
    circleText.Size = UDim2.new(1, -2, 1, -2)
    circleText.Position = UDim2.new(0, 1, 0, 1)
    circleText.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    circleText.Image = "rbxthumb://type=Asset&id=9933991033&w=150&h=150"
    circleText.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Fit
    -- was TextLabel, now image
    circleText.Parent = circle

    -- Continuous spin + RGB
    local rot, hue = 0, 0
    local conn
    conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not isScriptAlive or not circle or not circle.Parent then
            if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
            return
        end
        rot = (rot + 140 * dt) % 360
        circle.Rotation = rot
        hue = (hue + 0.45 * dt) % 1
        local rgb = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.95, 1)
        circleStroke.Color = rgb
        -- circle logo image
        stroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV((hue + 0.08) % 1, 0.9, 1)
    end)

    local divider = Instance.new("Frame")
    divider.Size = UDim2.new(0, 1.5, 0.7, 0)
    divider.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0.15, 0)
    divider.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
    divider.BorderSizePixel = 0
    divider.Parent = frame

    local overheadEGOLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    overheadEGOLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0.54, 0)
    overheadEGOLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.26, 0, 0.02, 0)
    overheadEGOLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    overheadEGOLabel.Text = "Eɢᴏ Eᴍᴘɪʀᴇ: Usᴇʀ"
    overheadEGOLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)
    overheadEGOLabel.TextScaled = true
    overheadEGOLabel.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    overheadEGOLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    overheadEGOLabel.Parent = frame

    local nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0.72, 0, 0.38, 0)
    nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0.26, 0, 0.57, 0)
    nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    nameLabel.Text = "@" .. player.Name
    nameLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    nameLabel.TextScaled = true
    nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
    nameLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    nameLabel.Parent = frame

    -- Keep billboard enabled state
    local renderConn
    renderConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not isScriptAlive or not character or not character.Parent or not head or not head.Parent then
            renderConn:Disconnect()
            return
        end
        billboard.Enabled = EGOState.tagsEnabled
    end)

    -- EGO PARTICLES
    local function createThunder(parent, offsetY, rate, speed, size)
        if not isScriptAlive or not parent or not parent.Parent then return end
        local att = Instance.new("Attachment")
        att.Name = "EGOAttachment"
        att.Position = Vector3.new(0, offsetY, 0)
        att.Parent = parent

        local thunder = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        thunder.Parent = att
        thunder.Texture = "rbxassetid://6101261905"
        thunder.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 50)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.8, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 140, 0))
        })
        thunder.LightEmission = 1
        thunder.LightInfluence = 0
        thunder.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 0.6),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3, size * 1.4),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7, size * 0.9),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        thunder.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 0.15),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        thunder.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6)
        thunder.Rate = rate
        thunder.Speed = NumberRange.new(speed, speed + 4)
        thunder.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(90, 90)
        thunder.RotSpeed = NumberRange.new(-250, 250)
        thunder.Rotation = NumberRange.new(0, 360)
        thunder.ZOffset = 0.4

        local glow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        glow.Parent = att
        glow.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(180, 140, 0))
        })
        glow.LightEmission = 1
        glow.LightInfluence = 0
        glow.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 2.0),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, size * 1.4),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.25),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.55),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        glow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.5)
        glow.Rate = rate * 0.7
        glow.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 2.5)
        glow.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(360, 360)

        local smoke = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
        smoke.Parent = att
        smoke.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(80, 60, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(40, 30, 0))
        })
        smoke.LightEmission = 0.2
        smoke.LightInfluence = 0.5
        smoke.Size = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, size * 0.8),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, size * 2.2),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
        })
        smoke.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.5),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.6, 0.8),
            NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
        })
        smoke.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8)
        smoke.Rate = rate * 0.4
        smoke.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3)
        smoke.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(45, 45)

        thunder.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        glow.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        smoke.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, thunder)
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, glow)
        table.insert(EGOState.emitters, smoke)
    end

    for _, obj in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name ~= "HumanoidRootPart" then
            createThunder(obj, 0, 60, 4, 1.2)
        end
    end
    createThunder(hrp, 0, 70, 5, 1.4)

    local groundAtt = Instance.new("Attachment")
    groundAtt.Name = "EGOAttachment"
    groundAtt.Position = Vector3.new(0, -3, 0)
    groundAtt.Parent = hrp

    local groundThunder = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter")
    groundThunder.Parent = groundAtt
    groundThunder.Texture = "rbxassetid://6101261905"
    groundThunder.Color = ColorSequence.new({
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 50)),
        ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, Color3.fromRGB(200, 160, 0))
    })
    groundThunder.LightEmission = 1
    groundThunder.LightInfluence = 0
    groundThunder.Size = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.4),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 1.4),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)
    })
    groundThunder.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.1),
        NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)
    })
    groundThunder.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.7)
    groundThunder.Rate = 75
    groundThunder.Speed = NumberRange.new(3, 6)
    groundThunder.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(110, 110)
    groundThunder.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
    groundThunder.Enabled = EGOState.EGOEnabled
    table.insert(EGOState.emitters, groundThunder)

    -- Idle Animation
    local idleAnim = Instance.new("Animation")
    idleAnim.Name = "EGOIdle"
    idleAnim.AnimationId = "rbxassetid://138665010911335"
    local idleTrack = animator:LoadAnimation(idleAnim)
    idleTrack.Priority = Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle
    idleTrack.Looped = true
    EGOState.currentTrack = idleTrack

    humanoid.Running:Connect(function(speed)
        if not isScriptAlive then return end
        if speed < 0.5 then
            if EGOState.animEnabled and not idleTrack.IsPlaying then
                idleTrack:Play(0.1)
            end
        else
            if idleTrack.IsPlaying then
                idleTrack:Stop(0.1)
            end
        end
    end)

    if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude < 0.1 and EGOState.animEnabled then
        idleTrack:Play(0.1)
    end
end

local function attachCharacterTracks(character)
    if not isScriptAlive or not character then return end
    task.spawn(setup, character)
end

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        local currentCharacter = player.Character
        if currentCharacter and currentCharacter.Parent then
            local head = currentCharacter:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head and not head:FindFirstChild("OverheadTag") then
                task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, currentCharacter)
            end
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
end)

if player.Character then
    task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, player.Character)
end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.spawn(attachCharacterTracks, char)
end)

print("✅ NARAK GANG EGO LOADED | Red Star Removed | Spinning RGB Circle Active")

]========]
    local fn, err = loadstring(EGOCode)
    if fn then
        local ok, e = pcall(fn)
        if ok then
            print("✅ NARAK GANG EGO AUTO-LOADED")
        else
            warn("[EGO EGO] Runtime error:", e)
        end
    else
        warn("[EGO EGO] Compile error:", err)
    end
end)

local colorOrder = {"GOLD","YELLOW","ORANGE","GREEN","LIME","CYAN","SKY","BLUE","PURPLE","VIOLET","PINK","HOTPINK","WHITE","SILVER","MINT","TEAL","TURQUOISE","PEACH","LAVENDER","ICE","INDIGO","RAINBOW"}

local function getEGOMainColor()
    if EGOState.currentEGOColor == "RAINBOW" then return Color3.fromHSV((tick()*0.28)%1, 0.85, 1) end
    local c = EGOColors[EGOState.currentEGOColor]
    return c and c.main or Color3.fromRGB(255,200,50)
end
local function getEGOParticleColors()
    local c = EGOColors[EGOState.currentEGOColor]
    return c and c.particles or EGOColors.GOLD.particles
end

_G.EGOKillSwitch = function()
    isScriptAlive = false
    local char = player.Character
    if char then
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if hum then
            local anim = hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
            if anim then
                for _,t in ipairs(anim:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
                    if t.Animation and (t.Animation.AnimationId=="rbxassetid://138665010911335" or t.Name=="EGOIdle") then t:Stop(0) t:Destroy() end
                end
            end
        end
        for _,c in ipairs(char:GetDescendants()) do
            if c.Name=="OverheadTag" or c.Name=="EGOAttachment" or c.Name=="EgoCircleLogo" then c:Destroy() end
        end
    end
    if EGOState.currentTrack then pcall(function() EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0) EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy() end) end
    if EGOState.emitters then for _,e in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do if e and e.Parent then e:Destroy() end end end
    if playerGui then local h=playerGui:FindFirstChild("EGOHUD") if h then h:Destroy() end end
end

local ringRot,ringHue=0,0
local brandWords={"13","EGO","EMPIRE","HUB"} local brandIdx=1 local brandTimers={1.0,1.0,1.2,1.0}
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
    if not spinRing or not spinRing.Parent then return end
    ringRot=(ringRot+100*dt)%360 spinRing.Rotation=ringRot spinContent.Rotation=0
    ringHue=(ringHue+0.35*dt)%1
    local rgb=Color3.fromHSV(ringHue,0.9,1)
    ringStroke.Color=rgb brandText.TextColor3=rgb innerGlow.Color=Color3.fromHSV((ringHue+0.5)%1,0.6,1)
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        task.wait(brandTimers[brandIdx] or 1)
        local t1=TweenService:Create(brandText,TweenInfo.new(0.2),{TextTransparency=1}) t1:Play() t1.Completed:Wait()
        brandIdx=brandIdx%#brandWords+1 brandText.Text=brandWords[brandIdx]
        brandText.TextSize=(brandWords[brandIdx]=="EMPIRE") and 8 or 10
        local t2=TweenService:Create(brandText,TweenInfo.new(0.25),{TextTransparency=0}) t2:Play()
    end
end)

local function playSound(id)
    if not isScriptAlive or not hud then return end
    local s=Instance.new("Sound") s.SoundId="rbxassetid://"..tostring(id) s.Volume=0.5 s.Parent=hud s:Play() Debris:AddItem(s,2)
end

local function createBox(name,text,withDot,interactive)
    local box=Instance.new("Frame") box.Name=name box.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X box.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,1,-4)
    box.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0) box.BackgroundTransparency=0.25 box.BorderSizePixel=0 box.Parent=bar
    Instance.new("UICorner",box).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,4)
    local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",box) stroke.Color=getEGOMainColor() stroke.Thickness=1
    local layout=Instance.new("UIListLayout",box) layout.FillDirection=Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    layout.VerticalAlignment=Enum.VerticalAlignment.Center layout.Padding=UDim.new(0,3)
    local padding=Instance.new("UIPadding",box) padding.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,6) padding.PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,6)
    local dot
    if withDot then
        dot=Instance.new("Frame") dot.Size=UDim2.new(0,5,0,5) dot.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,255,100)
        dot.BorderSizePixel=0 dot.Parent=box Instance.new("UICorner",dot).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    end
    local label=interactive and Instance.new("TextButton") or Instance.new("TextLabel")
    if interactive then label.AutoButtonColor=false end
    label.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.X label.Size=UDim2.new(0,0,1,0) label.BackgroundTransparency=1
    label.TextColor3=getEGOMainColor() label.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold label.TextSize=10 label.Text=text label.Parent=box
    return box,label,dot,stroke
end

local function refreshUIColors()
    local col=getEGOMainColor() barStroke.Color=col
    for _,child in ipairs(bar:GetChildren()) do
        if child:IsA("Frame") then
            local st=child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") if st then st.Color=col end
            for _,lbl in ipairs(child:GetChildren()) do
                if (lbl:IsA("TextLabel") or lbl:IsA("TextButton")) and lbl.Text~="" then lbl.TextColor3=col end
            end
        end
    end
end

local _,tagsLabel,tagsDot=createBox("TagsToggle","Tᴀɢs Øɴ",true,true)
tagsLabel.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not isScriptAlive then return end playSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.tagsEnabled=not EGOState.tagsEnabled
    tagsLabel.Text=EGOState.tagsEnabled and "Tᴀɢs Øɴ" or "Tᴀɢs Øғғ"
    tagsDot.BackgroundColor3=EGOState.tagsEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0,255,100) or Color3.fromRGB(120,80,0)
    local head=player.Character and player.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
    if head then local tag=head:FindFirstChild("OverheadTag") if tag then tag.Enabled=EGOState.tagsEnabled end end
end)

local morePanel,moreOpen=nil,false
local function applyEGOColor(cname)
    EGOState.currentEGOColor=cname refreshUIColors()
    local cols=getEGOParticleColors()
    for _,e in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do
        if e and e.Parent and cname~="RAINBOW" then
            pcall(function() e.Color=ColorSequence.new({
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,cols[1]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35,cols[2]),
                ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7,cols[3]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,cols[4])
            }) end)
        end
    end
end

local function createMorePanel()
    if morePanel and morePanel.Parent then morePanel:Destroy() end
    morePanel=Instance.new("Frame") morePanel.Name="EGOColorPanel" morePanel.Size=UDim2.new(0,230,0,0)
    morePanel.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y morePanel.Position=UDim2.new(1,-240,0,42)
    morePanel.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(10,10,18) morePanel.BackgroundTransparency=0.08
    morePanel.BorderSizePixel=0 morePanel.Visible=false morePanel.ZIndex=50 morePanel.Parent=hud
    Instance.new("UICorner",morePanel).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,10)
    local pst=Instance.new("UIStroke",morePanel) pst.Color=getEGOMainColor() pst.Thickness=1.5
    local pad=Instance.new("UIPadding",morePanel)
    pad.PaddingTop=UDim.new(0,8) pad.PaddingBottom=UDim.new(0,10) pad.PaddingLeft=UDim.new(0,8) pad.PaddingRight=UDim.new(0,8)
    local title=Instance.new("TextLabel",morePanel) title.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,20) title.BackgroundTransparency=1
    title.Text="🇦🇱  EGO COLORS" title.TextColor3=getEGOMainColor() title.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold title.TextSize=12 title.ZIndex=51
    local grid=Instance.new("Frame",morePanel) grid.Name="EGOColorGrid" grid.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,0,0)
    grid.Position=UDim2.new(0,0,0,26) grid.BackgroundTransparency=1 grid.AutomaticSize=Enum.AutomaticSize.Y grid.ZIndex=51
    local gl=Instance.new("UIGridLayout",grid) gl.CellSize=UDim2.new(0,68,0,22) gl.CellPadding=UDim2.new(0,5,0,5) gl.FillDirectionMaxCells=3
    for _,cname in ipairs(colorOrder) do
        local data=EGOColors[cname]
        local b=Instance.new("TextButton",grid)
        b.BackgroundColor3=data.main b.Text=(EGOState.currentEGOColor==cname and "✓ " or "")..cname
        b.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,14) b.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold b.TextSize=9 b.ZIndex=52
        Instance.new("UICorner",b).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,5)
        local bst=Instance.new("UIStroke",b) bst.Thickness=(EGOState.currentEGOColor==cname) and 2 or 0 bst.Color=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
        b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            playSound(107406973902030) applyEGOColor(cname)
            for _,child in ipairs(grid:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("TextButton") then
                    local nm=child.Text:gsub("✓ ","") child.Text=(nm==cname and "✓ " or "")..nm
                    local st=child:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke") if st then st.Thickness=(nm==cname) and 2 or 0 end
                end
            end
            morePanel.Visible=false moreOpen=false
        end)
    end
end
createMorePanel()
