-- ========== CLEAN SPAM (NO Start/Stop) ==========
local baseHeader="SCRIPTER]> AvEK💀//THE PRIME MEMBER ØF NARAK GANG🌃//EGO WALO MERE PYARE BACHE🌃// () DEDO 🌃// NAME EGO!? but REALITY [RND]🌃/WORST GANG EGO RND🌃..."
local function cleanName(name)
    if not name or name=="" then return "RYUK" end
    name=name:gsub("[%z\1-\127\194-\244][\128-\191]*",function(c) return (#c>1) and "" or c end)
    name=name:gsub("%s+"," "):match("^%s*(.-)%s*$") or ""
    if name=="" then return "RYUK" end
    return name
end

local spamPanel,nameBoxRef,targetBoxRef,statusLblRef,previewLblRef=nil,nil,nil,nil,nil
local function updateSpamPreview()
    if not nameBoxRef or not statusLblRef then return end
    local sName=cleanName(nameBoxRef.Text)
    local tName=(targetBoxRef and targetBoxRef.Text~="") and targetBoxRef.Text or "NO ONE"
    local header=baseHeader:gsub("AvEK",sName)
    local short=header if #short>55 then short=string.sub(short,1,52).."..." end
    if previewLblRef then previewLblRef.Text="PREVIEW: "..short end
    if EGOState.spamEnabled then
        statusLblRef.Text="● ACTIVE  |  "..sName.." → "..tName
        statusLblRef.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,230,100)
    else
        statusLblRef.Text="○ OFF"
        statusLblRef.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(160,160,180)
    end
end


local function createSpamPanel()
    if spamPanel and spamPanel.Parent then spamPanel:Destroy() end
    spamPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
    spamPanel.Name = "SpamConfigPanel"
    spamPanel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 195, 0, 0)
    spamPanel.AutomaticSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
    spamPanel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -205, 0, 42)
    spamPanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(12, 12, 20)
    spamPanel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
    spamPanel.BorderSizePixel = 0
    spamPanel.Visible = false
    spamPanel.ZIndex = 50
    spamPanel.Parent = hud
    Instance.new("UICorner", spamPanel).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 8)
    local st = Instance.new("UIStroke", spamPanel)
    st.Color = getEGOMainColor()
    st.Thickness = 1.4
    local pad = Instance.new("UIPadding", spamPanel)
    pad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
    pad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
    pad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
    local layout = Instance.new("UIListLayout", spamPanel)
    layout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)
    layout.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder

    local function makeLabel(txt)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel", spamPanel)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 14)
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Text = txt
        l.TextColor3 = getEGOMainColor()
        l.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
        l.TextSize = 11
        l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
        l.ZIndex = 51
        return l
    end
    local function makeBox(ph, default)
        local b = Instance.new("TextBox", spamPanel)
        b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26)
        b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(22, 22, 34)
        b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        b.PlaceholderText = ph
        b.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(120, 120, 145)
        b.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
        b.TextSize = 12
        b.Text = default or ""
        b.ClearTextOnFocus = false
        b.ZIndex = 51
        Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
        return b
    end

    makeLabel("UR. NAME?")
    nameBoxRef = makeBox("Your name...", player.DisplayName or "")
    makeLabel("TARGET NAME")
    targetBoxRef = makeBox("Target name...")

    statusLblRef = Instance.new("TextLabel", spamPanel)
    statusLblRef.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
    statusLblRef.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    statusLblRef.Text = "○ READY"
    statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
    statusLblRef.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    statusLblRef.TextSize = 11
    statusLblRef.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
    statusLblRef.ZIndex = 51

    local row = Instance.new("Frame", spamPanel)
    row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
    row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    row.ZIndex = 51
    local rl = Instance.new("UIListLayout", row)
    rl.FillDirection = Enum.FillDirection.Horizontal
    rl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 6)
    rl.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center

    local startBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    startBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0)
    startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
    startBtn.Text = "START"
    startBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    startBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    startBtn.TextSize = 12
    startBtn.ZIndex = 52
    Instance.new("UICorner", startBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local stopBtn = Instance.new("TextButton", row)
    stopBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0.48, 0, 1, 0)
    stopBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 40, 40)
    stopBtn.Text = "STOP"
    stopBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    stopBtn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    stopBtn.TextSize = 12
    stopBtn.ZIndex = 52
    Instance.new("UICorner", stopBtn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)

    local spamRunning = false
    local replies = {
        "TMX MEH GOAT","TMX MEH LEGEND","TMX MEH HALWA","TMX MEH FAN",
        "TMX MEH CAR","TMX MEH KING","TMX MEH BOSS","TMX MEH STAR",
        "TMX MEH FIRE","TMX MEH ICE","RYUK ON TOP","EGO ON TOP",
        "LEAVE MARDE","BHAG JA","ROO MAT","SUN JA"
    }
    local randomEmojis = {"😈","👑","🩸","🤟","🧵","🎯","🤩","🌠","🌟","🌈","🌞","🔥","🇦🇱","🌊","💎","📚","🧊"}

    local function sendMsg(msg)
        if #msg > 195 then msg = string.sub(msg, 1, 195) end
        pcall(function()
            if TextChatService.ChatVersion == Enum.ChatVersion.TextChatService then
                local ch = TextChatService.TextChannels:FindFirstChild("RBXGeneral")
                if ch then ch:SendAsync(msg) end
            else
                local ev = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild("DefaultChatSystemChatEvents")
                if ev and ev:FindFirstChild("SayMessageRequest") then
                    ev.SayMessageRequest:FireServer(msg, "All")
                end
            end
        end)
    end

    startBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        playSound(107406973902030)
        if spamRunning then return end
        local sName = cleanName(nameBoxRef.Text)
        local tName = (targetBoxRef.Text ~= "" and targetBoxRef.Text) or "NO ONE"
        spamRunning = true
        startBtn.Text = "● RUN"
        startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 120, 50)
        statusLblRef.Text = "● ACTIVE | " .. sName .. " → " .. tName
        statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 230, 100)

        task.spawn(function()
            local i = 1
            while spamRunning and isScriptAlive and EGOState.spamEnabled do
                local reply = replies[i] or "TMX MEH GOAT"
                local emoji = randomEmojis[math.random(1, #randomEmojis)]
                local header = baseHeader:gsub("AvEK", sName):gsub("🌃", emoji)
                local msg = header .. " [" .. tName .. "] " .. reply
                sendMsg(msg)
                i = i % #replies + 1
                task.wait(1.4)
            end
            spamRunning = false
            if startBtn and startBtn.Parent then
                startBtn.Text = "START"
                startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
            end
            if statusLblRef and statusLblRef.Parent then
                statusLblRef.Text = "○ READY"
                statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 180)
            end
        end)
    end)

    stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        playSound(107406973902030)
        spamRunning = false
        startBtn.Text = "START"
        startBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 160, 70)
        statusLblRef.Text = "○ STOPPED"
        statusLblRef.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 100, 100)
    end)
end

createSpamPanel()

local _,spamLabel,spamDot=createBox("SpamToggle","Sᴘᴀᴍ Øғғ",true,true)
spamLabel.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not isScriptAlive then return end
    playSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.spamEnabled=not EGOState.spamEnabled
    spamLabel.Text=EGOState.spamEnabled and "Sᴘᴀᴍ Øɴ" or "Sᴘᴀᴍ Øғғ"
    spamDot.BackgroundColor3=EGOState.spamEnabled and Color3.fromRGB(0,255,100) or Color3.fromRGB(120,80,0)
    if spamPanel then spamPanel.Visible=EGOState.spamEnabled end
    updateSpamPreview()
end)

-- HUD toggle
local isTweening=false
logoClick.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    if not isScriptAlive or isTweening then return end isTweening=true playSound(107406973902030)
    EGOState.hudVisible=not EGOState.hudVisible
    local target=EGOState.hudVisible and UDim2.new(0,0,0,0) or UDim2.new(0,850,0,0)
    local tw=TweenService:Create(sliderFrame,TweenInfo.new(0.4,Enum.EasingStyle.Cubic),{Position=target})
    tw:Play() tw.Completed:Connect(function() isTweening=false end)
end)

RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
    if not isScriptAlive then return end
    if dt>0 then fpsLabel.Text="Fᴘs: "..math.floor(1/dt) end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        pcall(function()
            local v=StatsService.Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
            pingLabel.Text="Pɪɴɢ: "..math.floor(v).."ᴍs"
        end)
        local dt=DateTime.now():ToLocalTime() local h=dt.Hour
        local ampm=h>=12 and "PM" or "AM" if h>12 then h=h-12 elseif h==0 then h=12 end
        timeLabel.Text=string.format("%02d:%02d %s",h,dt.Minute,ampm) task.wait(1)
    end
end)
task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        if EGOState.currentEGOColor=="RAINBOW" and EGOState.EGOEnabled then
            local h=(tick()*0.28)%1
            local c1=Color3.fromHSV(h,0.85,1) local c2=Color3.fromHSV((h+0.25)%1,0.85,1)
            local c3=Color3.fromHSV((h+0.5)%1,0.85,1) local c4=Color3.fromHSV((h+0.75)%1,0.85,1)
            for _,e in ipairs(EGOState.emitters) do
                if e and e.Parent then pcall(function() e.Color=ColorSequence.new({
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,c1),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35,c2),
                    ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7,c3),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,c4)
                }) end) end
            end
        end
        task.wait(0.08)
    end
end)

-- Character setup (overhead + EGO)
local function setup(character)
    if not isScriptAlive or not character or not character.Parent then return end
    local head=character:WaitForChild("Head",4)
    local humanoid=character:WaitForChild("Humanoid",4)
    local hrp=character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart",4)
    if not head or not humanoid or not hrp then return end
    local animator=humanoid:WaitForChild("Animator",4) or humanoid:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
    if not animator then return end

    for _,c in ipairs(head:GetChildren()) do if c.Name=="OverheadTag" or c.Name=="EgoCircleLogo" then c:Destroy() end end
    for _,c in ipairs(character:GetDescendants()) do if c.Name=="EGOAttachment" then c:Destroy() end end
    EGOState.emitters={}
    if EGOState.currentTrack then pcall(function() EGOState.currentTrack:Stop(0) EGOState.currentTrack:Destroy() end) EGOState.currentTrack=nil end

    local billboard=Instance.new("BillboardGui") billboard.Name="OverheadTag" billboard.Size=UDim2.new(0,150,0,38)
    billboard.StudsOffset=Vector3.new(0,2.9,0) billboard.AlwaysOnTop=true billboard.MaxDistance=math.huge billboard.Parent=head
    local frame=Instance.new("Frame") frame.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0) frame.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(0,0,0)
    frame.BackgroundTransparency=0.35 frame.BorderSizePixel=0 frame.Parent=billboard
    Instance.new("UICorner",frame).CornerRadius=UDim.new(0,6)
    local stroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",frame) stroke.Color=getEGOMainColor() stroke.Thickness=1.8

    local ovRing=Instance.new("Frame") ovRing.Name="EgoCircleLogo" ovRing.Size=UDim2.new(0,26,0,26)
    ovRing.Position=UDim2.new(0,4,0.5,-13) ovRing.BackgroundTransparency=1 ovRing.Parent=frame
    Instance.new("UICorner",ovRing).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    local ovRingStroke=Instance.new("UIStroke",ovRing) ovRingStroke.Thickness=2.2 ovRingStroke.Color=getEGOMainColor()

    local ovContent=Instance.new("Frame") ovContent.Size=UDim2.new(0,23,0,23) ovContent.Position=UDim2.new(0,5.5,0.5,-11.5)
    ovContent.BackgroundColor3=Color3.fromRGB(8,8,14) ovContent.BackgroundTransparency=0.15 ovContent.BorderSizePixel=0 ovContent.Parent=frame
    Instance.new("UICorner",ovContent).CornerRadius=UDim.new(1,0)
    local ovText=Instance.new("TextLabel") ovText.Size=UDim2.new(1,0,1,0) ovText.BackgroundTransparency=1
    ovText.Text="13" ovText.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBlack ovText.TextSize=9 ovText.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    ovText.TextScaled=true ovText.Parent=ovContent

    local ovRot,ovHue,ovIdx=0,0,1 local ovWords={"13","EGO","EMPIRE","HUB"}
    task.spawn(function()
        while isScriptAlive and ovContent and ovContent.Parent do
            task.wait(1.1)
            local t1=TweenService:Create(ovText,TweenInfo.new(0.18),{TextTransparency=1}) t1:Play() t1.Completed:Wait()
            ovIdx=ovIdx%#ovWords+1 ovText.Text=ovWords[ovIdx]
            local t2=TweenService:Create(ovText,TweenInfo.new(0.2),{TextTransparency=0}) t2:Play()
        end
    end)
    local ovConn
    ovConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not isScriptAlive or not ovRing or not ovRing.Parent then if ovConn then ovConn:Disconnect() end return end
        ovRot=(ovRot+130*dt)%360 ovRing.Rotation=ovRot ovContent.Rotation=0
        ovHue=(ovHue+0.4*dt)%1 local rgb=Color3.fromHSV(ovHue,0.9,1)
        ovRingStroke.Color=rgb stroke.Color=getEGOMainColor()
    end)

    local divider=Instance.new("Frame") divider.Size=UDim2.new(0,1.5,0.7,0) divider.Position=UDim2.new(0,34,0.15,0)
    divider.BackgroundColor3=getEGOMainColor() divider.BorderSizePixel=0 divider.Parent=frame
    local overheadLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel") overheadLabel.Size=UDim2.new(0.72,0,0.54,0)
    overheadLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0.26,0,0.02,0) overheadLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
    overheadLabel.Text="Eɢᴏ Eᴍᴘɪʀᴇ: Usᴇʀ" overheadLabel.TextColor3=getEGOMainColor()
    overheadLabel.TextScaled=true overheadLabel.Font=Enum.Font.GothamBold overheadLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left overheadLabel.Parent=frame
    local nameLabel=Instance.new("TextLabel") nameLabel.Size=UDim2.new(0.72,0,0.38,0)
    nameLabel.Position=UDim2.new(0.26,0,0.57,0) nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency=1
    nameLabel.Text="@"..player.Name nameLabel.TextColor3=Color3.fromRGB(255,255,255)
    nameLabel.TextScaled=true nameLabel.Font=Enum.Font.Gotham nameLabel.TextXAlignment=Enum.TextXAlignment.Left nameLabel.Parent=frame

    local rConn
    rConn=RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not isScriptAlive or not character.Parent or not head.Parent then rConn:Disconnect() return end
        billboard.Enabled=EGOState.tagsEnabled
    end)

    local function createThunder(parent,offsetY,rate,speed,size)
        if not isScriptAlive or not parent or not parent.Parent then return end
        local att=Instance.new("Attachment") att.Name="EGOAttachment" att.Position=Vector3.new(0,offsetY,0) att.Parent=parent
        local cols=getEGOParticleColors()
        local thunder=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter") thunder.Parent=att thunder.Texture="rbxassetid://6101261905"
        thunder.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,cols[1]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.35,cols[2]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7,cols[3]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,cols[4])})
        thunder.LightEmission=1 thunder.LightInfluence=0
        thunder.Size=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,size*0.6),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.3,size*1.4),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.7,size*0.9),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0)})
        thunder.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4,0.15),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)})
        thunder.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(0.3,0.6) thunder.Rate=rate thunder.Speed=NumberRange.new(speed,speed+4)
        thunder.SpreadAngle=Vector2.new(90,90) thunder.RotSpeed=NumberRange.new(-250,250) thunder.Rotation=NumberRange.new(0,360) thunder.ZOffset=0.4
        thunder.Enabled=EGOState.EGOEnabled table.insert(EGOState.emitters,thunder)
        local glow=Instance.new("ParticleEmitter") glow.Parent=att
        glow.Color=ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0,cols[1]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,cols[2]),ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1,cols[4])})
        glow.LightEmission=1 glow.LightInfluence=0
        glow.Size=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,size*2),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4,size*1.4),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,0)})
        glow.Transparency=NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0,0.25),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5,0.55),NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1,1)})
        glow.Lifetime=NumberRange.new(0.3,0.5) glow.Rate=rate*0.7 glow.Speed=NumberRange.new(1,2.5) glow.SpreadAngle=Vector2.new(360,360)
        glow.Enabled=EGOState.EGOEnabled table.insert(EGOState.emitters,glow)
    end
    for _,obj in ipairs(character:GetChildren()) do
        if obj:IsA("BasePart") and obj.Name~="HumanoidRootPart" then createThunder(obj,0,55,4,1.15) end
    end
    createThunder(hrp,0,65,5,1.35)

    local idleAnim=Instance.new("Animation") idleAnim.Name="EGOIdle" idleAnim.AnimationId="rbxassetid://138665010911335"
    local idleTrack=animator:LoadAnimation(idleAnim) idleTrack.Priority=Enum.AnimationPriority.Idle idleTrack.Looped=true
    EGOState.currentTrack=idleTrack
    humanoid.Running:Connect(function(speed)
        if not isScriptAlive then return end
        if speed<0.5 then if EGOState.animEnabled and not idleTrack.IsPlaying then idleTrack:Play(0.1) end
        else if idleTrack.IsPlaying then idleTrack:Stop(0.1) end end
    end)
    if humanoid.MoveDirection.Magnitude<0.1 and EGOState.animEnabled then idleTrack:Play(0.1) end
end

task.spawn(function()
    while isScriptAlive do
        local char=player.Character
        if char and char.Parent then
            local head=char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            if head and not head:FindFirstChild("OverheadTag") then task.spawn(setup,char) end
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
    end
end)
if player.Character then task.spawn(setup,player.Character) end
player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(c) task.spawn(setup,c) end)

print("✅ NARAK GANG EGO FINAL | Clean Spam (no Start/Stop) | 22 Colors | No Red")

    if fn then
        local ok, e = pcall(fn)

            print("✅ NARAK GANG EGO PRO AUTO-LOADED")

            warn("[EGO PRO] Error:", e)
        end
    else

    end
end)
